+func expectChar(s string, c uint8) (string, error) {
+	if len(s) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: unexpected EOF", errDatetime)
+	} else if s[0] != c {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: unexpected character %c", errDatetime, s[0])
+	}
+	return s[1:], nil
+}
+
+func parseUint(s string, chars int, maxValue uint, label string) (uint, string, error) {
+	if len(s) < chars {
+		return 0, "", fmt.Errorf("%w: unexpected EOF", errDatetime)
+	}
+	v, err := strconv.ParseUint(s[0:chars], 10, 0)
+	if err != nil {
+		return 0, "", fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid %v", errDatetime, label)
+	} else if v > uint64(maxValue) {
+		return 0, "", fmt.Errorf("%w: %v is greater than %v", errDatetime, label, maxValue)
+	}
+	return uint(v), s[chars:], nil
+}
+
+// checkValidDay ensures that the given day is valid for the given month in the given year.
+func checkValidDay(year int, month, day uint) error {
+	t := time.Date(year, time.Month(month), int(day), 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
+
+	// Don't allow wrapping: https://github.com/cedar-policy/rfcs/pull/94
+	_, tmonth, tday := t.Date()
+	if time.Month(month) != tmonth || int(day) != tday {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid date", errDatetime)
+	}
+
+	return nil
+}

   1 | PORT
   2 | 
   3 | ## Commit Summary
   4 | - **SHA:** c1177bf
   5 | - **Date:** 2026-01-29T11:38:05-08:00
   6 | - **Title:** types: add support RFC 110 expanded year format
   7 | - **Description:** Adds support for a 9-digit expanded year datetime format as specified in Cedar RFC 110. This allows datetimes far in the past or future (beyond ±9999) to be expressed using a leading `+` or `-` sign followed by exactly 9 year digits (e.g., `+000010000-01-01T00:00:00.000Z`).
   8 | 
   9 | ## Semantic Analysis
  10 | 
  11 | ### What changed in Go
  12 | 1. **`ParseDatetime` (types/datetime.go):** Full rewrite of the parsing logic to:
  13 |    - Detect leading `+` or `-` to switch to 9-digit expanded year mode vs. standard 4-digit year mode.
  14 |    - Parse 9-digit years with a sign prefix when in expanded mode.
  15 |    - Validate parsed dates against `maxDatetime` / `minDatetime` overflow bounds.
  16 |    - Changed error messages to use "is greater than N" pattern instead of "is out of range".
  17 |    - Added helper functions: `expectChar`, `parseUint`, `checkValidDay`.
  18 | 2. **`MarshalCedar` / `MarshalJSON` (types/datetime.go):** Serialization must now emit the expanded format (`+YYYYYYYYY-...`) when the year is outside `[0, 9999]` (i.e., < 0 or > 9999). Years 0–9999 continue to use 4-digit format.
  19 | 3. **Overflow bounds:** Introduced `maxDatetime` and `minDatetime` sentinels (the extremes of int64 milliseconds), and `ParseDatetime` now rejects timestamps that would overflow them.
  20 | 4. **Tests (types/datetime_test.go):** New parse error cases for expanded year edge cases, range overflow, and unexpected EOF. New `MarshalCedar` / `MarshalJSON` table tests covering negative years, years > 9999, etc.
  21 | 
  22 | ### Impact on C# codebase
  23 | The `Datetime` Cedar type in C# needs the same three behavioral changes:
  24 | - **Parsing:** accept `+YYYYYYYYY-...` and `-YYYYYYYYY-...` formats.
  25 | - **Serialization:** emit expanded format when year < 0 or year > 9999.
  26 | - **Range check:** reject timestamps that overflow `long` milliseconds.
  27 | 
  28 | ## Port Tasks
  29 | 
  30 | ### 1. Locate the C# Datetime type & parser
  31 | - Target file: `src/Cedar.Types/` — look for a file named `Datetime.cs` or similar (may be `CedarDatetime.cs`, `DatetimeValue.cs`, etc.).
  32 | - Find `ParseDatetime` / `TryParse` equivalent method.
  33 | 
  34 | ### 2. Update `ParseDatetime` (parsing)
  35 | - Before reading year digits, inspect the first character:
  36 |   - If `'+'` or `'-'`: consume it, set `yearSign`, expect exactly **9** decimal digits for the year, set `yearMax = 999_999_999`.
  37 |   - Otherwise: expect exactly **4** decimal digits, `yearMax = 9999`.
  38 | - After assembling the `DateTimeOffset`, check it is within the representable `long` millisecond range:
  39 |   - `maxDatetime ≈ year 292278994-08-17T07:12:55.807Z`
  40 |   - `minDatetime ≈ year -292275055-05-17T16:47:04.192Z`
  41 |   - Return an error if out of range.
  42 | - Update error messages to match new Go wording: `"second is greater than 59"`, `"minute is greater than 59"`, etc. (only if error messages are tested / part of public API).
  43 | 
  44 | ### 3. Update `MarshalCedar` / serialization
  45 | - When formatting a `Datetime` back to its Cedar string representation:
  46 |   - If year is in `[0, 9999]`: use standard `yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffZ` (4-digit year, zero-padded).
  47 |   - If year < 0 or year > 9999: use `±YYYYYYYYY-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffZ` (sign + 9-digit zero-padded year).
  48 | - The Go implementation uses `%09d` for the 9-digit zero-padded year.
  49 | 
  50 | ### 4. Add / update tests in `test/Cedar.Tests/`
  51 | - Find the existing datetime parsing test file (e.g., `DatetimeTests.cs`).
  52 | - Add parse-success cases:
  53 |   - `"+000010000-01-01T00:00:00.000Z"` → year 10000
  54 |   - `"-000000001-01-01T00:00:00.000Z"` → year -1
  55 |   - `"+292278994-08-17T07:12:55.807Z"` → max representable
  56 |   - `"-292275055-05-17T16:47:04.192Z"` → min representable
  57 | - Add parse-error cases:
  58 |   - `"+"` → unexpected EOF
  59 |   - `"-"` → unexpected EOF
  60 |   - `"+1234-01-01"` → invalid year (only 4 digits when 9 expected)
  61 |   - `"+292278994-08-17T07:12:55.808Z"` → timestamp out of range
  62 |   - `"-292275055-05-17T16:47:04.191Z"` → timestamp out of range
  63 | - Add serialization cases:
  64 |   - year 10000 → `datetime("+000010000-01-01T00:00:00.000Z")`
  65 |   - year -1 → `datetime("-000000001-01-01T00:00:00.000Z")`
  66 |   - year 0 → `datetime("0000-01-01T00:00:00.000Z")` (4-digit, no sign)
  67 |   - year 9999 → `datetime("9999-12-31T00:00:00.000Z")` (4-digit, no sign)
  68 | 
  69 | ### 5. JSON round-trip
  70 | - Verify that `MarshalJSON` / `System.Text.Json` serialization also uses the expanded format in the `"arg"` field for years outside [0, 9999], consistent with `MarshalCedar`.
  71 | - Add a JSON round-trip test for a 5-digit-year datetime (e.g., year 10000).
  72 | 
  73 | ## Notes
  74 | - .NET's `DateTimeOffset` supports years 1–9999 natively. For years outside that range, consider using `System.DateTimeOffset` with a custom offset or storing the raw millisecond `long` value and computing year/month/day manually for formatting — matching how the Go code stores `int64` millis and formats via `time.Unix`.
  75 | - If the C# `Datetime` type already stores a raw `long` milliseconds value (like Go), formatting the expanded year will require manual calendar arithmetic for years outside `DateTimeOffset`'s range.
  76 | - The `checkValidDay` logic (no day-wrapping) should already be covered by .NET's `DateTimeOffset` constructor throwing on invalid dates — verify this is the case or add an explicit check.

   1 | using System;
   2 | using System.Globalization;
   3 | 
   4 | namespace Cedar.Types;
   5 | 
   6 | public sealed record CedarDatetime(long Value) : CedarValue
   7 | {
   8 |     public static CedarDatetime Parse(string value)
   9 |     {
  10 |         ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(value);
  11 | 
  12 |         if (value.Length < 10)
  13 |         {
  14 |             throw new FormatException("Datetime values must contain at least a full date.");
  15 |         }
  16 | 
  17 |         int year = ParseDigits(value, 0, 4, "year");
  18 |         ExpectCharacter(value, 4, '-');
  19 |         int month = ParseDigits(value, 5, 2, "month");
  20 |         ExpectCharacter(value, 7, '-');
  21 |         int day = ParseDigits(value, 8, 2, "day");
  22 | 
  23 |         ValidateDate(year, month, day);
  24 | 
  25 |         if (value.Length == 10)
  26 |         {
  27 |             return new CedarDatetime(new DateTimeOffset(year, month, day, 0, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero).ToUnixTimeMilliseconds());
  28 |         }
  29 | 
  30 |         if (value.Length < 20)
  31 |         {
  32 |             throw new FormatException("Datetime values must contain a full time component.");
  33 |         }
  34 | 
  35 |         ExpectCharacter(value, 10, 'T');
  36 |         int hour = ParseDigits(value, 11, 2, "hour");
  37 |         ExpectCharacter(value, 13, ':');
  38 |         int minute = ParseDigits(value, 14, 2, "minute");
  39 |         ExpectCharacter(value, 16, ':');
  40 |         int second = ParseDigits(value, 17, 2, "second");
  41 | 
  42 |         if (hour > 23)
  43 |         {
  44 |             throw new FormatException("Hour is out of range.");
  45 |         }
  46 | 
  47 |         if (minute > 59)
  48 |         {
  49 |             throw new FormatException("Minute is out of range.");
  50 |         }
  51 | 
  52 |         if (second > 59)
  53 |         {
  54 |             throw new FormatException("Second is out of range.");
  55 |         }
  56 | 
  57 |         int milliseconds = 0;
  58 |         int trailerOffset = 19;
  59 | 
  60 |         if (value[19] == '.')
  61 |         {
  62 |             if (value.Length < 23)
  63 |             {
  64 |                 throw new FormatException("Millisecond component is invalid.");
  65 |             }
  66 | 
  67 |             milliseconds = ParseDigits(value, 20, 3, "millisecond");
  68 |             trailerOffset = 23;
  69 |         }
  70 | 
  71 |         if (value.Length == trailerOffset)
  72 |         {
  73 |             throw new FormatException("Datetime values must include a time zone designator.");
  74 |         }
  75 | 
  76 |         TimeSpan offset = value[trailerOffset] switch
  77 |         {
  78 |             'Z' => ReadUtcTrailer(value, trailerOffset),
  79 |             '+' => ReadOffset(value, trailerOffset, 1),
  80 |             '-' => ReadOffset(value, trailerOffset, -1),
  81 |             _ => throw new FormatException("Invalid time zone designator.")
  82 |         };
  83 | 
  84 |         DateTimeOffset timestamp = new(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, milliseconds, TimeSpan.Zero);
  85 |         return new CedarDatetime(timestamp.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds() - (long)offset.TotalMilliseconds);
  86 |     }
  87 | 
  88 |     public static CedarDatetime FromDateTimeOffset(DateTimeOffset value)
  89 |     {
  90 |         return new CedarDatetime(value.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds());
  91 |     }
  92 | 
  93 |     public DateTimeOffset ToDateTimeOffset()
  94 |     {
  95 |         return DateTimeOffset.FromUnixTimeMilliseconds(Value).ToUniversalTime();
  96 |     }
  97 | 
  98 |     public override string MarshalCedar()
  99 |     {
 100 |         return "datetime(\"" + ToDateTimeOffset().ToString("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss.fff'Z'", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "\")";
 101 |     }
 102 | 
 103 |     public override int GetHashCode()
 104 |     {
 105 |         return CedarHash.ForInt64(nameof(CedarDatetime), Value);
 106 |     }
 107 | 
 108 |     private static void ExpectCharacter(string value, int index, char expected)
 109 |     {
 110 |         if (value.Length <= index || value[index] != expected)
 111 |         {
 112 |             throw new FormatException($"Expected '{expected}' at position {index}.");
 113 |         }
 114 |     }
 115 | 
 116 |     private static int ParseDigits(string value, int offset, int length, string component)
 117 |     {
 118 |         if (value.Length < offset + length)
 119 |         {
 120 |             throw new FormatException($"Invalid {component} component.");
 121 |         }
 122 | 
 123 |         ReadOnlySpan<char> span = value.AsSpan(offset, length);
 124 |         foreach (char character in span)
 125 |         {
 126 |             if (!char.IsAsciiDigit(character))
 127 |             {
 128 |                 throw new FormatException($"Invalid {component} component.");
 129 |             }
 130 |         }
 131 | 
 132 |         return int.Parse(span, NumberStyles.None, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
 133 |     }
 134 | 
 135 |     private static TimeSpan ReadUtcTrailer(string value, int offset)
 136 |     {
 137 |         if (value.Length != offset + 1)
 138 |         {
 139 |             throw new FormatException("Unexpected trailing characters after the time zone designator.");
 140 |         }
 141 | 
 142 |         return TimeSpan.Zero;
 143 |     }
 144 | 
 145 |     private static TimeSpan ReadOffset(string value, int offset, int sign)
 146 |     {
 147 |         if (value.Length != offset + 5)
 148 |         {
 149 |             throw new FormatException("Time zone offsets must use the +hhmm or -hhmm form.");
 150 |         }
 151 | 
 152 |         int hours = ParseDigits(value, offset + 1, 2, "time zone hour");
 153 |         int minutes = ParseDigits(value, offset + 3, 2, "time zone minute");
 154 | 
 155 |         if (hours > 23)
 156 |         {
 157 |             throw new FormatException("Time zone offset hours are out of range.");
 158 |         }
 159 | 
 160 |         if (minutes > 59)
 161 |         {
 162 |             throw new FormatException("Time zone offset minutes are out of range.");
 163 |         }
 164 | 
 165 |         TimeSpan magnitude = new(hours, minutes, 0);
 166 |         return TimeSpan.FromTicks(sign * magnitude.Ticks);
 167 |     }
 168 | 
 169 |     private static void ValidateDate(int year, int month, int day)
 170 |     {
 171 |         if (month is < 1 or > 12)
 172 |         {
 173 |             throw new FormatException("Month is out of range.");
 174 |         }
 175 | 
 176 |         int daysInMonth = DateTime.DaysInMonth(year, month);
 177 |         if (day is < 1 or > 31)
 178 |         {
 179 |             throw new FormatException("Day is out of range.");
 180 |         }
 181 | 
 182 |         if (day > daysInMonth)
 183 |         {
 184 |             throw new FormatException("Date is invalid.");
 185 |         }
 186 |     }
 187 | }

  40 |             case CedarBool boolean:
  41 |                 writer.WriteBooleanValue(boolean.Value);
  42 |                 break;
  43 |             case CedarLong integer:
  44 |                 writer.WriteNumberValue(integer.Value);
  45 |                 break;
  46 |             case CedarString text:
  47 |                 writer.WriteStringValue(text.Value);
  48 |                 break;
  49 |             case CedarDecimal decimalValue:
  50 |                 WriteExtension(writer, "decimal", decimalValue.MarshalCedar()[9..^2]);
  51 |                 break;
  52 |             case CedarDatetime datetime:
  53 |                 WriteExtension(writer, "datetime", datetime.MarshalCedar()[10..^2]);
  54 |                 break;
  55 |             case CedarDuration duration:
  56 |                 WriteExtension(writer, "duration", duration.MarshalCedar()[10..^2]);
  57 |                 break;
  58 |             case CedarIpAddress ipAddress:
  59 |                 WriteExtension(writer, "ip", ipAddress.MarshalCedar()[4..^2]);
  60 |                 break;
  61 |             case CedarPattern pattern:
  62 |                 WriteExtension(writer, "pattern", pattern.ToPatternText());
  63 |                 break;
  64 |             case CedarSet set:
  65 |                 writer.WriteStartArray();
  66 |                 foreach (ICedarData item in set)
  67 |                 {
  68 |                     WriteValue(writer, item);
  69 |                 }
  70 | 
  71 |                 writer.WriteEndArray();
  72 |                 break;
  73 |             case CedarRecord record:
  74 |                 writer.WriteStartObject();
  75 |                 foreach ((CedarString key, ICedarData item) in record)
  76 |                 {
  77 |                     writer.WritePropertyName(key.Value);
  78 |                     WriteValue(writer, item);
  79 |                 }
  80 | 
  81 |                 writer.WriteEndObject();
  82 |                 break;
  83 |             case EntityUid entityUid:
  84 |                 EntityUidJsonConverter.WriteExplicit(writer, entityUid);
  85 |                 break;
  86 |             default:
  87 |                 throw new JsonException($"Unsupported Cedar JSON type '{value.GetType().FullName}'.");
  88 |         }
  89 |     }
  90 | 
  91 |     private static CedarLong ReadLong(JsonElement element)
  92 |     {
  93 |         if (!element.TryGetInt64(out long value))
  94 |         {
  95 |             throw new JsonException("JSON numbers must fit in a signed 64-bit integer.");
  96 |         }
  97 | 
  98 |         return new CedarLong(value);
  99 |     }
 100 | 
 101 |     private static CedarSet ReadSet(JsonElement element)
 102 |     {
 103 |         List<ICedarData> values = [];
 104 |         foreach (JsonElement item in element.EnumerateArray())
 105 |         {
 106 |             values.Add(ReadElement(item));
 107 |         }
 108 | 
 109 |         return new CedarSet(values);
 110 |     }
 111 | 
 112 |     private static ICedarData ReadObject(JsonElement element)
 113 |     {
 114 |         if (EntityUidJsonConverter.TryReadElement(element, out EntityUid? entityUid))
 115 |         {
 116 |             return entityUid!;
 117 |         }
 118 | 
 119 |         if (TryReadExtension(element, out ICedarData? extension))
 120 |         {
 121 |             return extension!;
 122 |         }
 123 | 
 124 |         RecordMap values = [];
 125 |         foreach (JsonProperty property in element.EnumerateObject())
 126 |         {
 127 |             values.Add(new CedarString(property.Name), ReadElement(property.Value));
 128 |         }
 129 | 
 130 |         return new CedarRecord(values);
 131 |     }
 132 | 
 133 |     private static bool TryReadExtension(JsonElement element, out ICedarData? value)
 134 |     {
 135 |         value = null;
 136 | 
 137 |         if (!TryGetExtensionElement(element, out JsonElement extension))
 138 |         {
 139 |             return false;
 140 |         }
 141 | 
 142 |         if (!extension.TryGetProperty("fn", out JsonElement functionElement) || functionElement.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.String)
 143 |         {
 144 |             throw new JsonException("Extension values must contain a string fn property.");
 145 |         }
 146 | 
 147 |         if (!extension.TryGetProperty("arg", out JsonElement argumentElement) || argumentElement.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.String)
 148 |         {
 149 |             throw new JsonException("Extension values must contain a string arg property.");
 150 |         }
 151 | 
 152 |         string function = functionElement.GetString()!;
 153 |         string argument = argumentElement.GetString()!;
 154 | 
 155 |         value = function switch
 156 |         {
 157 |             "decimal" => CedarDecimal.Parse(argument),
 158 |             "datetime" => CedarDatetime.Parse(argument),
 159 |             "duration" => CedarDuration.Parse(argument),
 160 |             "ip" => CedarIpAddress.Parse(argument),
 161 |             "pattern" => CedarPattern.Parse(argument),
 162 |             _ => throw new JsonException($"Unsupported Cedar extension '{function}'.")
 163 |         };
 164 | 
 165 |         return true;
 166 |     }
 167 | 
 168 |     private static bool TryGetExtensionElement(JsonElement element, out JsonElement extension)
 169 |     {
 170 |         if (element.TryGetProperty("__extn", out JsonElement explicitExtension))
 171 |         {
 172 |             if (explicitExtension.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Object
 173 |                 && explicitExtension.TryGetProperty("fn", out JsonElement explicitFunction)
 174 |                 && explicitFunction.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.String
 175 |                 && explicitExtension.TryGetProperty("arg", out JsonElement explicitArgument)
 176 |                 && explicitArgument.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.String)
 177 |             {
 178 |                 extension = explicitExtension;
 179 |                 return true;
 180 |             }
 181 | 
 182 |             extension = default;
 183 |             return false;
 184 |         }
 185 | 
 186 |         if (element.TryGetProperty("fn", out JsonElement functionElement)
 187 |             && functionElement.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.String
 188 |             && element.TryGetProperty("arg", out JsonElement argumentElement)
 189 |             && argumentElement.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.String)
 190 |         {
 191 |             extension = element;
 192 |             return true;
 193 |         }
 194 | 
 195 |         extension = default;
 196 |         return false;
 197 |     }
 198 | 
 199 |     private static void WriteExtension(Utf8JsonWriter writer, string function, string argument)
 200 |     {
 201 |         writer.WriteStartObject();
 202 |         writer.WritePropertyName("__extn");
 203 |         writer.WriteStartObject();
 204 |         writer.WriteString("fn", function);
 205 |         writer.WriteString("arg", argument);
 206 |         writer.WriteEndObject();
 207 |         writer.WriteEndObject();
 208 |     }
 209 | }

   1 | using System;
   2 | using Cedar.Core.Internal.Consts;
   3 | using Cedar.Core.Internal.Eval;
   4 | using Cedar.Types;
   5 | 
   6 | namespace Cedar.Core.Internal.Extensions;
   7 | 
   8 | internal static class DatetimeExtensions
   9 | {
  10 |     public static ICedarData ToDate(ICedarData[] args)
  11 |     {
  12 |         CedarDatetime value = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]);
  13 |         long millis = value.Value - (value.Value % CedarConsts.MillisPerDay);
  14 |         return new CedarDatetime(millis);
  15 |     }
  16 | 
  17 |     public static ICedarData ToTime(ICedarData[] args)
  18 |     {
  19 |         CedarDatetime value = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]);
  20 |         return new CedarDuration(value.Value % CedarConsts.MillisPerDay);
  21 |     }
  22 | 
  23 |     public static ICedarData Offset(ICedarData[] args)
  24 |     {
  25 |         CedarDatetime datetime = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]);
  26 |         CedarDuration duration = TypeConversion.ValueToDuration(args[1]);
  27 | 
  28 |         try
  29 |         {
  30 |             return new CedarDatetime(checked(datetime.Value + duration.Value));
  31 |         }
  32 |         catch (OverflowException)
  33 |         {
  34 |             throw new EvalException($"{EvalErrors.Overflow} while attempting to compute datetime offset");
  35 |         }
  36 |     }
  37 | 
  38 |     public static ICedarData DurationSince(ICedarData[] args)
  39 |     {
  40 |         CedarDatetime left = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]);
  41 |         CedarDatetime right = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[1]);
  42 | 
  43 |         try
  44 |         {
  45 |             return new CedarDuration(checked(left.Value - right.Value));
  46 |         }
  47 |         catch (OverflowException)
  48 |         {
  49 |             throw new EvalException($"{EvalErrors.Overflow} while attempting to compute datetime duration");
  50 |         }
  51 |     }
  52 | 
  53 |     public static ICedarData DaysInMonth(ICedarData[] args)
  54 |     {
  55 |         DateTimeOffset value = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).ToDateTimeOffset();
  56 |         return new CedarLong(DateTime.DaysInMonth(value.Year, value.Month));
  57 |     }

  70 |     {

  72 |     }

  78 |     }

   1 | using System;
   2 | using System.Globalization;
   3 | 
   4 | namespace Cedar.Types;
   5 | 
   6 | public sealed record CedarDatetime(long Value) : CedarValue
   7 | {
   8 |     public static CedarDatetime Parse(string value)
   9 |     {
  10 |         ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(value);
  11 | 
  12 |         if (value.Length < 10)
  13 |         {
  14 |             throw new FormatException("Datetime values must contain at least a full date.");
  15 |         }
  16 | 
  17 |         int year = ParseDigits(value, 0, 4, "year");
  18 |         ExpectCharacter(value, 4, '-');
  19 |         int month = ParseDigits(value, 5, 2, "month");
  20 |         ExpectCharacter(value, 7, '-');
  21 |         int day = ParseDigits(value, 8, 2, "day");
  22 | 
  23 |         ValidateDate(year, month, day);
  24 | 
  25 |         if (value.Length == 10)
  26 |         {
  27 |             return new CedarDatetime(new DateTimeOffset(year, month, day, 0, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero).ToUnixTimeMilliseconds());
  28 |         }
  29 | 
  30 |         if (value.Length < 20)
  31 |         {
  32 |             throw new FormatException("Datetime values must contain a full time component.");
  33 |         }
  34 | 
  35 |         ExpectCharacter(value, 10, 'T');
  36 |         int hour = ParseDigits(value, 11, 2, "hour");
  37 |         ExpectCharacter(value, 13, ':');
  38 |         int minute = ParseDigits(value, 14, 2, "minute");
  39 |         ExpectCharacter(value, 16, ':');
  40 |         int second = ParseDigits(value, 17, 2, "second");
  41 | 
  42 |         if (hour > 23)
  43 |         {
  44 |             throw new FormatException("Hour is out of range.");
  45 |         }
  46 | 
  47 |         if (minute > 59)
  48 |         {
  49 |             throw new FormatException("Minute is out of range.");
  50 |         }
  51 | 
  52 |         if (second > 59)
  53 |         {
  54 |             throw new FormatException("Second is out of range.");
  55 |         }
  56 | 
  57 |         int milliseconds = 0;
  58 |         int trailerOffset = 19;
  59 | 
  60 |         if (value[19] == '.')
  61 |         {
  62 |             if (value.Length < 23)
  63 |             {
  64 |                 throw new FormatException("Millisecond component is invalid.");
  65 |             }
  66 | 
  67 |             milliseconds = ParseDigits(value, 20, 3, "millisecond");
  68 |             trailerOffset = 23;
  69 |         }
  70 | 
  71 |         if (value.Length == trailerOffset)
  72 |         {
  73 |             throw new FormatException("Datetime values must include a time zone designator.");
  74 |         }
  75 | 
  76 |         TimeSpan offset = value[trailerOffset] switch
  77 |         {
  78 |             'Z' => ReadUtcTrailer(value, trailerOffset),
  79 |             '+' => ReadOffset(value, trailerOffset, 1),
  80 |             '-' => ReadOffset(value, trailerOffset, -1),
  81 |             _ => throw new FormatException("Invalid time zone designator.")
  82 |         };
  83 | 
  84 |         DateTimeOffset timestamp = new(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, milliseconds, TimeSpan.Zero);
  85 |         return new CedarDatetime(timestamp.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds() - (long)offset.TotalMilliseconds);
  86 |     }
  87 | 
  88 |     public static CedarDatetime FromDateTimeOffset(DateTimeOffset value)
  89 |     {
  90 |         return new CedarDatetime(value.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds());
  91 |     }
  92 | 
  93 |     public DateTimeOffset ToDateTimeOffset()
  94 |     {
  95 |         return DateTimeOffset.FromUnixTimeMilliseconds(Value).ToUniversalTime();
  96 |     }
  97 | 
  98 |     public override string MarshalCedar()
  99 |     {
 100 |         return "datetime(\"" + ToDateTimeOffset().ToString("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss.fff'Z'", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "\")";
 101 |     }
 102 | 
 103 |     public override int GetHashCode()
 104 |     {
 105 |         return CedarHash.ForInt64(nameof(CedarDatetime), Value);
 106 |     }
 107 | 
 108 |     private static void ExpectCharacter(string value, int index, char expected)
 109 |     {
 110 |         if (value.Length <= index || value[index] != expected)
 111 |         {
 112 |             throw new FormatException($"Expected '{expected}' at position {index}.");
 113 |         }
 114 |     }
 115 | 
 116 |     private static int ParseDigits(string value, int offset, int length, string component)
 117 |     {
 118 |         if (value.Length < offset + length)
 119 |         {
 120 |             throw new FormatException($"Invalid {component} component.");
 121 |         }
 122 | 
 123 |         ReadOnlySpan<char> span = value.AsSpan(offset, length);
 124 |         foreach (char character in span)
 125 |         {
 126 |             if (!char.IsAsciiDigit(character))
 127 |             {
 128 |                 throw new FormatException($"Invalid {component} component.");
 129 |             }
 130 |         }
 131 | 
 132 |         return int.Parse(span, NumberStyles.None, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
 133 |     }
 134 | 
 135 |     private static TimeSpan ReadUtcTrailer(string value, int offset)
 136 |     {
 137 |         if (value.Length != offset + 1)
 138 |         {
 139 |             throw new FormatException("Unexpected trailing characters after the time zone designator.");
 140 |         }
 141 | 
 142 |         return TimeSpan.Zero;
 143 |     }
 144 | 
 145 |     private static TimeSpan ReadOffset(string value, int offset, int sign)
 146 |     {
 147 |         if (value.Length != offset + 5)
 148 |         {
 149 |             throw new FormatException("Time zone offsets must use the +hhmm or -hhmm form.");
 150 |         }
 151 | 
 152 |         int hours = ParseDigits(value, offset + 1, 2, "time zone hour");
 153 |         int minutes = ParseDigits(value, offset + 3, 2, "time zone minute");
 154 | 
 155 |         if (hours > 23)
 156 |         {
 157 |             throw new FormatException("Time zone offset hours are out of range.");
 158 |         }
 159 | 
 160 |         if (minutes > 59)
 161 |         {
 162 |             throw new FormatException("Time zone offset minutes are out of range.");
 163 |         }
 164 | 
 165 |         TimeSpan magnitude = new(hours, minutes, 0);
 166 |         return TimeSpan.FromTicks(sign * magnitude.Ticks);
 167 |     }
 168 | 
 169 |     private static void ValidateDate(int year, int month, int day)
 170 |     {
 171 |         if (month is < 1 or > 12)
 172 |         {
 173 |             throw new FormatException("Month is out of range.");
 174 |         }
 175 | 
 176 |         int daysInMonth = DateTime.DaysInMonth(year, month);
 177 |         if (day is < 1 or > 31)
 178 |         {
 179 |             throw new FormatException("Day is out of range.");
 180 |         }
 181 | 
 182 |         if (day > daysInMonth)
 183 |         {
 184 |             throw new FormatException("Date is invalid.");
 185 |         }
 186 |     }
 187 | }

   1 | using System;
   2 | using Cedar.Core.Internal.Consts;
   3 | using Cedar.Core.Internal.Eval;
   4 | using Cedar.Types;
   5 | 
   6 | namespace Cedar.Core.Internal.Extensions;
   7 | 
   8 | internal static class DatetimeExtensions
   9 | {
  10 |     public static ICedarData ToDate(ICedarData[] args)
  11 |     {
  12 |         CedarDatetime value = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]);
  13 |         long millis = value.Value - (value.Value % CedarConsts.MillisPerDay);
  14 |         return new CedarDatetime(millis);
  15 |     }
  16 | 
  17 |     public static ICedarData ToTime(ICedarData[] args)
  18 |     {
  19 |         CedarDatetime value = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]);
  20 |         return new CedarDuration(value.Value % CedarConsts.MillisPerDay);
  21 |     }
  22 | 
  23 |     public static ICedarData Offset(ICedarData[] args)
  24 |     {
  25 |         CedarDatetime datetime = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]);
  26 |         CedarDuration duration = TypeConversion.ValueToDuration(args[1]);
  27 | 
  28 |         try
  29 |         {
  30 |             return new CedarDatetime(checked(datetime.Value + duration.Value));
  31 |         }
  32 |         catch (OverflowException)
  33 |         {
  34 |             throw new EvalException($"{EvalErrors.Overflow} while attempting to compute datetime offset");
  35 |         }
  36 |     }
  37 | 
  38 |     public static ICedarData DurationSince(ICedarData[] args)
  39 |     {
  40 |         CedarDatetime left = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]);
  41 |         CedarDatetime right = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[1]);
  42 | 
  43 |         try
  44 |         {
  45 |             return new CedarDuration(checked(left.Value - right.Value));
  46 |         }
  47 |         catch (OverflowException)
  48 |         {
  49 |             throw new EvalException($"{EvalErrors.Overflow} while attempting to compute datetime duration");
  50 |         }
  51 |     }
  52 | 
  53 |     public static ICedarData DaysInMonth(ICedarData[] args)
  54 |     {
  55 |         DateTimeOffset value = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).ToDateTimeOffset();
  56 |         return new CedarLong(DateTime.DaysInMonth(value.Year, value.Month));
  57 |     }
  58 | 
  59 |     public static ICedarData Year(ICedarData[] args)
  60 |     {
  61 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).ToDateTimeOffset().Year);
  62 |     }
  63 | 
  64 |     public static ICedarData Month(ICedarData[] args)
  65 |     {
  66 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).ToDateTimeOffset().Month);
  67 |     }
  68 | 
  69 |     public static ICedarData Day(ICedarData[] args)
  70 |     {
  71 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).ToDateTimeOffset().Day);
  72 |     }
  73 | 
  74 |     public static ICedarData DayOfWeek(ICedarData[] args)
  75 |     {
  76 |         DayOfWeek day = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).ToDateTimeOffset().DayOfWeek;
  77 |         return new CedarLong(day == System.DayOfWeek.Sunday ? 7 : (int)day);
  78 |     }
  79 | 
  80 |     public static ICedarData DayOfYear(ICedarData[] args)
  81 |     {
  82 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).ToDateTimeOffset().DayOfYear);
  83 |     }
  84 | 
  85 |     public static ICedarData Hour(ICedarData[] args)
  86 |     {
  87 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).ToDateTimeOffset().Hour);
  88 |     }
  89 | 
  90 |     public static ICedarData Minute(ICedarData[] args)
  91 |     {
  92 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).ToDateTimeOffset().Minute);
  93 |     }
  94 | 
  95 |     public static ICedarData Second(ICedarData[] args)
  96 |     {
  97 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).ToDateTimeOffset().Second);
  98 |     }
  99 |

   1 | using System;
   2 | using System.Globalization;
   3 | using Cedar.Core.Internal.Consts;
   4 | using Cedar.Internal.DateTimeSupport;
   5 | 
   6 | namespace Cedar.Types;
   7 | 
   8 | public sealed record CedarDatetime(long Value) : CedarValue
   9 | {
  10 |     public static CedarDatetime Parse(string value)
  11 |     {
  12 |         ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(value);
  13 | 
  14 |         if (value.Length == 0)
  15 |         {
  16 |             throw new FormatException("Datetime values must not be empty.");
  17 |         }
  18 | 
  19 |         int cursor = 0;
  20 |         int yearSign = 1;
  21 |         int yearDigits = 4;
  22 | 
  23 |         if (value[cursor] is '+' or '-')
  24 |         {
  25 |             yearDigits = 9;
  26 |             if (value[cursor] == '-')
  27 |             {
  28 |                 yearSign = -1;
  29 |             }
  30 | 
  31 |             cursor++;
  32 |         }
  33 | 
  34 |         int yearMagnitude = ParseDigits(value, ref cursor, yearDigits, "year");
  35 |         int year = checked(yearSign * yearMagnitude);
  36 | 
  37 |         ExpectCharacter(value, ref cursor, '-');
  38 |         int month = ParseDigits(value, ref cursor, 2, "month");
  39 |         ExpectCharacter(value, ref cursor, '-');
  40 |         int day = ParseDigits(value, ref cursor, 2, "day");
  41 | 
  42 |         ValidateDate(year, month, day);
  43 | 
  44 |         if (cursor == value.Length)
  45 |         {
  46 |             return CreateFromParts(year, month, day, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);
  47 |         }
  48 | 
  49 |         ExpectCharacter(value, ref cursor, 'T');
  50 |         int hour = ParseDigits(value, ref cursor, 2, "hour");
  51 |         ExpectCharacter(value, ref cursor, ':');
  52 |         int minute = ParseDigits(value, ref cursor, 2, "minute");
  53 |         ExpectCharacter(value, ref cursor, ':');
  54 |         int second = ParseDigits(value, ref cursor, 2, "second");
  55 | 
  56 |         if (hour > 23)
  57 |         {
  58 |             throw new FormatException("Hour is greater than 23.");
  59 |         }
  60 | 
  61 |         if (minute > 59)
  62 |         {
  63 |             throw new FormatException("Minute is greater than 59.");
  64 |         }
  65 | 
  66 |         if (second > 59)
  67 |         {
  68 |             throw new FormatException("Second is greater than 59.");
  69 |         }
  70 | 
  71 |         int milliseconds = 0;
  72 | 
  73 |         if (cursor < value.Length && value[cursor] == '.')
  74 |         {
  75 |             cursor++;
  76 |             milliseconds = ParseDigits(value, ref cursor, 3, "millisecond");
  77 |         }
  78 | 
  79 |         if (cursor == value.Length)
  80 |         {
  81 |             throw new FormatException("Datetime values must include a time zone designator.");
  82 |         }
  83 | 
  84 |         long offsetMilliseconds = value[cursor] switch
  85 |         {
  86 |             'Z' => ReadUtcTrailer(value, ref cursor),
  87 |             '+' => ReadOffset(value, ref cursor, 1),
  88 |             '-' => ReadOffset(value, ref cursor, -1),
  89 |             _ => throw new FormatException("Invalid time zone designator.")
  90 |         };
  91 | 
  92 |         if (cursor != value.Length)
  93 |         {
  94 |             throw new FormatException("Unexpected trailing characters after the time zone designator.");
  95 |         }
  96 | 
  97 |         return CreateFromParts(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, milliseconds, offsetMilliseconds);
  98 |     }
  99 | 
 100 |     public static CedarDatetime FromDateTimeOffset(DateTimeOffset value)
 101 |     {
 102 |         return new CedarDatetime(value.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds());
 103 |     }
 104 | 
 105 |     public DateTimeOffset ToDateTimeOffset()
 106 |     {
 107 |         return DateTimeOffset.FromUnixTimeMilliseconds(Value).ToUniversalTime();
 108 |     }
 109 | 
 110 |     internal GregorianDateTimeParts GetParts()
 111 |     {
 112 |         return GregorianDateTime.FromUnixMilliseconds(Value);
 113 |     }
 114 | 
 115 |     public override string MarshalCedar()
 116 |     {
 117 |         GregorianDateTimeParts parts = GetParts();
 118 |         string year = FormatYear(parts.Year);
 119 | 
 120 |         return string.Create(
 121 |             CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
 122 |             $"datetime(\"{year}-{parts.Month:D2}-{parts.Day:D2}T{parts.Hour:D2}:{parts.Minute:D2}:{parts.Second:D2}.{parts.Millisecond:D3}Z\")");
 123 |     }
 124 | 
 125 |     public override int GetHashCode()
 126 |     {
 127 |         return CedarHash.ForInt64(nameof(CedarDatetime), Value);
 128 |     }
 129 | 
 130 |     private static CedarDatetime CreateFromParts(int year, int month, int day, int hour, int minute, int second, int millisecond, long offsetMilliseconds)
 131 |     {
 132 |         try
 133 |         {
 134 |             long milliseconds = GregorianDateTime.ToUnixMilliseconds(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, millisecond);
 135 |             return new CedarDatetime(checked(milliseconds - offsetMilliseconds));
 136 |         }
 137 |         catch (OverflowException)
 138 |         {
 139 |             throw new FormatException("Timestamp is out of range.");
 140 |         }
 141 |     }
 142 | 
 143 |     private static string FormatYear(int year)
 144 |     {
 145 |         if (year is >= 0 and <= 9999)
 146 |         {
 147 |             return year.ToString("D4", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
 148 |         }
 149 | 
 150 |         long absoluteYear = year < 0 ? -(long)year : year;
 151 |         string sign = year < 0 ? "-" : "+";
 152 |         return sign + absoluteYear.ToString("D9", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
 153 |     }
 154 | 
 155 |     private static void ExpectCharacter(string value, ref int cursor, char expected)
 156 |     {
 157 |         if (value.Length <= cursor || value[cursor] != expected)
 158 |         {
 159 |             throw new FormatException($"Expected '{expected}' at position {cursor}.");
 160 |         }
 161 | 
 162 |         cursor++;
 163 |     }
 164 | 
 165 |     private static int ParseDigits(string value, ref int cursor, int length, string component)
 166 |     {
 167 |         if (value.Length < cursor + length)
 168 |         {
 169 |             throw new FormatException($"Invalid {component} component.");
 170 |         }
 171 | 
 172 |         int parsed = 0;
 173 |         for (int index = 0; index < length; index++)
 174 |         {
 175 |             char character = value[cursor + index];
 176 |             if (!char.IsAsciiDigit(character))
 177 |             {
 178 |                 throw new FormatException($"Invalid {component} component.");
 179 |             }
 180 | 
 181 |             parsed = checked(parsed * 10 + (character - '0'));
 182 |         }
 183 | 
 184 |         cursor += length;
 185 |         return parsed;
 186 |     }
 187 | 
 188 |     private static long ReadUtcTrailer(string value, ref int cursor)
 189 |     {
 190 |         cursor++;
 191 |         return 0;
 192 |     }
 193 | 
 194 |     private static long ReadOffset(string value, ref int cursor, int sign)
 195 |     {
 196 |         cursor++;
 197 | 
 198 |         if (value.Length != cursor + 4)
 199 |         {
 200 |             throw new FormatException("Time zone offsets must use the +hhmm or -hhmm form.");
 201 |         }
 202 | 
 203 |         int hours = ParseDigits(value, ref cursor, 2, "time zone hour");
 204 |         int minutes = ParseDigits(value, ref cursor, 2, "time zone minute");
 205 | 
 206 |         if (hours > 23)
 207 |         {
 208 |             throw new FormatException("Time zone offset hours are greater than 23.");
 209 |         }
 210 | 
 211 |         if (minutes > 59)
 212 |         {
 213 |             throw new FormatException("Time zone offset minutes are greater than 59.");
 214 |         }
 215 | 
 216 |         long magnitude = (hours * CedarConsts.MillisPerHour) + (minutes * CedarConsts.MillisPerMinute);
 217 |         return sign * magnitude;
 218 |     }
 219 | 
 220 |     private static void ValidateDate(int year, int month, int day)
 221 |     {
 222 |         if (month is < 1 or > 12)
 223 |         {
 224 |             throw new FormatException("Month is greater than 12.");
 225 |         }
 226 | 
 227 |         if (day is < 1 or > 31)
 228 |         {
 229 |             throw new FormatException("Day is greater than 31.");
 230 |         }
 231 | 
 232 |         int daysInMonth = GregorianDateTime.DaysInMonth(year, month);
 233 |         if (day > daysInMonth)
 234 |         {
 235 |             throw new FormatException("Date is invalid.");
 236 |         }
 237 |     }
 238 | }

   1 | using System;
   2 | using Cedar.Core.Internal.Consts;
   3 | 
   4 | namespace Cedar.Internal.DateTimeSupport;
   5 | 
   6 | internal readonly record struct GregorianDateTimeParts(
   7 |     int Year,
   8 |     int Month,
   9 |     int Day,
  10 |     int Hour,
  11 |     int Minute,
  12 |     int Second,
  13 |     int Millisecond)
  14 | {
  15 |     public int DayOfYear => GregorianDateTime.GetDayOfYear(Year, Month, Day);
  16 | 
  17 |     public int IsoDayOfWeek => GregorianDateTime.GetIsoDayOfWeek(Year, Month, Day);
  18 | }
  19 | 
  20 | internal static class GregorianDateTime
  21 | {
  22 |     public static long ToUnixMilliseconds(int year, int month, int day, int hour, int minute, int second, int millisecond)
  23 |     {
  24 |         long days = DaysFromCivil(year, month, day);
  25 | 
  26 |         return checked(
  27 |             days * CedarConsts.MillisPerDay
  28 |             + (hour * CedarConsts.MillisPerHour)
  29 |             + (minute * CedarConsts.MillisPerMinute)
  30 |             + (second * CedarConsts.MillisPerSecond)
  31 |             + millisecond);
  32 |     }
  33 | 
  34 |     public static GregorianDateTimeParts FromUnixMilliseconds(long value)
  35 |     {
  36 |         long days = FloorDiv(value, CedarConsts.MillisPerDay);
  37 |         long timeOfDay = value - (days * CedarConsts.MillisPerDay);
  38 | 
  39 |         (int year, int month, int day) = CivilFromDays(days);
  40 | 
  41 |         int hour = (int)(timeOfDay / CedarConsts.MillisPerHour);
  42 |         timeOfDay %= CedarConsts.MillisPerHour;
  43 | 
  44 |         int minute = (int)(timeOfDay / CedarConsts.MillisPerMinute);
  45 |         timeOfDay %= CedarConsts.MillisPerMinute;
  46 | 
  47 |         int second = (int)(timeOfDay / CedarConsts.MillisPerSecond);
  48 |         int millisecond = (int)(timeOfDay % CedarConsts.MillisPerSecond);
  49 | 
  50 |         return new GregorianDateTimeParts(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, millisecond);
  51 |     }
  52 | 
  53 |     public static bool IsLeapYear(int year)
  54 |     {
  55 |         return PositiveMod(year, 4) == 0
  56 |             && (PositiveMod(year, 100) != 0 || PositiveMod(year, 400) == 0);
  57 |     }
  58 | 
  59 |     public static int DaysInMonth(int year, int month)
  60 |     {
  61 |         return month switch
  62 |         {
  63 |             1 or 3 or 5 or 7 or 8 or 10 or 12 => 31,
  64 |             4 or 6 or 9 or 11 => 30,
  65 |             2 => IsLeapYear(year) ? 29 : 28,
  66 |             _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(month))
  67 |         };
  68 |     }
  69 | 
  70 |     public static int GetDayOfYear(int year, int month, int day)
  71 |     {
  72 |         int[] commonYearOffsets = [0, 31, 59, 90, 120, 151, 181, 212, 243, 273, 304, 334];
  73 |         int dayOfYear = commonYearOffsets[month - 1] + day;
  74 | 
  75 |         if (month > 2 && IsLeapYear(year))
  76 |         {
  77 |             dayOfYear++;
  78 |         }
  79 | 
  80 |         return dayOfYear;
  81 |     }
  82 | 
  83 |     public static int GetIsoDayOfWeek(int year, int month, int day)
  84 |     {
  85 |         long days = DaysFromCivil(year, month, day);
  86 |         return (int)(PositiveMod(days + 3, 7) + 1);
  87 |     }
  88 | 
  89 |     public static long DaysFromCivil(int year, int month, int day)
  90 |     {
  91 |         long adjustedYear = month <= 2 ? year - 1L : year;
  92 |         long era = FloorDiv(adjustedYear, 400);
  93 |         long yearOfEra = adjustedYear - (era * 400);
  94 |         long monthPrime = month > 2 ? month - 3L : month + 9L;
  95 |         long dayOfYear = ((153 * monthPrime) + 2) / 5 + day - 1L;
  96 |         long dayOfEra = yearOfEra * 365 + yearOfEra / 4 - yearOfEra / 100 + dayOfYear;
  97 | 
  98 |         return era * 146097 + dayOfEra - 719468;
  99 |     }
 100 | 
 101 |     public static (int Year, int Month, int Day) CivilFromDays(long days)
 102 |     {
 103 |         long z = days + 719468;
 104 |         long era = FloorDiv(z, 146097);
 105 |         long dayOfEra = z - (era * 146097);
 106 |         long yearOfEra = (dayOfEra - dayOfEra / 1460 + dayOfEra / 36524 - dayOfEra / 146096) / 365;
 107 |         long year = yearOfEra + (era * 400);
 108 |         long dayOfYear = dayOfEra - (365 * yearOfEra + yearOfEra / 4 - yearOfEra / 100);
 109 |         long monthPrime = (5 * dayOfYear + 2) / 153;
 110 |         int day = (int)(dayOfYear - ((153 * monthPrime) + 2) / 5 + 1);
 111 |         int month = (int)(monthPrime < 10 ? monthPrime + 3 : monthPrime - 9);
 112 |         year += month <= 2 ? 1 : 0;
 113 | 
 114 |         return ((int)year, month, day);
 115 |     }
 116 | 
 117 |     private static long FloorDiv(long value, long divisor)
 118 |     {
 119 |         long quotient = value / divisor;
 120 |         long remainder = value % divisor;
 121 | 
 122 |         if (remainder != 0 && ((remainder > 0) != (divisor > 0)))
 123 |         {
 124 |             quotient--;
 125 |         }
 126 | 
 127 |         return quotient;
 128 |     }
 129 | 
 130 |     private static long PositiveMod(long value, long modulus)
 131 |     {
 132 |         long remainder = value % modulus;
 133 |         return remainder < 0 ? remainder + modulus : remainder;
 134 |     }
 135 | }

   1 | using System;
   2 | using Cedar.Core.Internal.Consts;
   3 | using Cedar.Core.Internal.Eval;
   4 | using Cedar.Internal.DateTimeSupport;
   5 | using Cedar.Types;
   6 | 
   7 | namespace Cedar.Core.Internal.Extensions;
   8 | 
   9 | internal static class DatetimeExtensions
  10 | {
  11 |     public static ICedarData ToDate(ICedarData[] args)
  12 |     {
  13 |         CedarDatetime value = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]);
  14 |         long millis = value.Value - (value.Value % CedarConsts.MillisPerDay);
  15 |         return new CedarDatetime(millis);
  16 |     }
  17 | 
  18 |     public static ICedarData ToTime(ICedarData[] args)
  19 |     {
  20 |         CedarDatetime value = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]);
  21 |         return new CedarDuration(value.Value % CedarConsts.MillisPerDay);
  22 |     }
  23 | 
  24 |     public static ICedarData Offset(ICedarData[] args)
  25 |     {
  26 |         CedarDatetime datetime = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]);
  27 |         CedarDuration duration = TypeConversion.ValueToDuration(args[1]);
  28 | 
  29 |         try
  30 |         {
  31 |             return new CedarDatetime(checked(datetime.Value + duration.Value));
  32 |         }
  33 |         catch (OverflowException)
  34 |         {
  35 |             throw new EvalException($"{EvalErrors.Overflow} while attempting to compute datetime offset");
  36 |         }
  37 |     }
  38 | 
  39 |     public static ICedarData DurationSince(ICedarData[] args)
  40 |     {
  41 |         CedarDatetime left = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]);
  42 |         CedarDatetime right = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[1]);
  43 | 
  44 |         try
  45 |         {
  46 |             return new CedarDuration(checked(left.Value - right.Value));
  47 |         }
  48 |         catch (OverflowException)
  49 |         {
  50 |             throw new EvalException($"{EvalErrors.Overflow} while attempting to compute datetime duration");
  51 |         }
  52 |     }
  53 | 
  54 |     public static ICedarData DaysInMonth(ICedarData[] args)
  55 |     {
  56 |         GregorianDateTimeParts value = TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).GetParts();
  57 |         return new CedarLong(GregorianDateTime.DaysInMonth(value.Year, value.Month));
  58 |     }
  59 | 
  60 |     public static ICedarData Year(ICedarData[] args)
  61 |     {
  62 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).GetParts().Year);
  63 |     }
  64 | 
  65 |     public static ICedarData Month(ICedarData[] args)
  66 |     {
  67 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).GetParts().Month);
  68 |     }
  69 | 
  70 |     public static ICedarData Day(ICedarData[] args)
  71 |     {
  72 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).GetParts().Day);
  73 |     }
  74 | 
  75 |     public static ICedarData DayOfWeek(ICedarData[] args)
  76 |     {
  77 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).GetParts().IsoDayOfWeek);
  78 |     }
  79 | 
  80 |     public static ICedarData DayOfYear(ICedarData[] args)
  81 |     {
  82 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).GetParts().DayOfYear);
  83 |     }
  84 | 
  85 |     public static ICedarData Hour(ICedarData[] args)
  86 |     {
  87 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).GetParts().Hour);
  88 |     }
  89 | 
  90 |     public static ICedarData Minute(ICedarData[] args)
  91 |     {
  92 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).GetParts().Minute);
  93 |     }
  94 | 
  95 |     public static ICedarData Second(ICedarData[] args)
  96 |     {
  97 |         return new CedarLong(TypeConversion.ValueToDatetime(args[0]).GetParts().Second);
  98 |     }
  99 | 
 100 |     public static ICedarData Millisecond(ICedarData[] args)

 183 |     [Fact]
 184 |     public void ToDate_StripsTimeComponent()
 185 |     {
 186 |         CedarDatetime dt = CedarDatetime.Parse("2024-06-15T13:30:45Z");
 187 |         ICedarData result = Invoke("toDate", dt);
 188 |         CedarDatetime date = Assert.IsType<CedarDatetime>(result);
 189 |         System.DateTimeOffset dto = date.ToDateTimeOffset();
 190 |         Assert.Equal(0, dto.Hour);
 191 |         Assert.Equal(0, dto.Minute);
 192 |         Assert.Equal(0, dto.Second);
 193 |     }
 194 | 
 195 |     [Fact]
 196 |     public void ToTime_ReturnsTimeDuration()
 197 |     {
 198 |         CedarDatetime dt = CedarDatetime.Parse("2024-06-15T13:30:45Z");
 199 |         ICedarData result = Invoke("toTime", dt);
 200 |         CedarDuration duration = Assert.IsType<CedarDuration>(result);
 201 |         Assert.True(duration.Value > 0);
 202 |     }
 203 | 
 204 |     [Fact]
 205 |     public void Offset_AddsDuration()
 206 |     {
 207 |         CedarDatetime dt = CedarDatetime.Parse("2024-01-01T00:00:00Z");
 208 |         CedarDuration dur = CedarDuration.Parse("1h");
 209 |         ICedarData result = Invoke("offset", dt, dur);
 210 |         CedarDatetime offsetDt = Assert.IsType<CedarDatetime>(result);
 211 |         Assert.Equal(dt.Value + dur.Value, offsetDt.Value);
 212 |     }
 213 | 
 214 |     [Fact]
 215 |     public void DurationSince_ComputesDifference()
 216 |     {
 217 |         CedarDatetime dt1 = CedarDatetime.Parse("2024-01-02T00:00:00Z");
 218 |         CedarDatetime dt2 = CedarDatetime.Parse("2024-01-01T00:00:00Z");
 219 |         ICedarData result = Invoke("durationSince", dt1, dt2);
 220 |         CedarDuration duration = Assert.IsType<CedarDuration>(result);
 221 |         Assert.Equal(86400000L, duration.Value);
 222 |     }

 228 |

 232 |         Assert.Equal(new CedarLong(15), Invoke("day", dt));
 233 |         Assert.Equal(new CedarLong(4), Invoke("dayOfWeek", dt));
 234 |         Assert.Equal(new CedarLong(167), Invoke("dayOfYear", dt));
 235 |         Assert.Equal(new CedarLong(13), Invoke("hour", dt));
 236 |         Assert.Equal(new CedarLong(30), Invoke("minute", dt));
 237 |         Assert.Equal(new CedarLong(45), Invoke("second", dt));
